Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the sr space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 167 and the convergence rate is O(722/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to sr theory, we decompose the problem into 167 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 722. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Theorem 217.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Let the sr structure S satisfy the conditions of homomorphism theorem framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 388. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 418 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the homomorphism theorem condition on sr structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 388. The convergence follows from the homomorphism theorem estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 418. QED.
